{"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Johnson amassed enough support to win the speakership because of his allegiance to Trumpian ideals, earning him the nickname on the far right of “Maga Mike”.","type":"example"},{"text":"MeidasTouch host Ben Meiselas reports on Republican Speaker of the House MAGA Mike becoming the worse messenger for the Trump shutdown as every single time he speaks he makes things much weirder and much worse and Meiselas speaks with Democratic Congressman Jared Moskowitz who mercilessly destroys MAGA Mike for his behavior.","type":"example"},{"text":"Perhaps that’s why Trump, who used a social media post to help drive a dagger into Majority Whip Tom Emmer’s candidacy on Tuesday, used another to boost the man his loyalists call “MAGA Mike.”","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Mike Johnson, American lawyer and 56th speaker of the House of Representatives."],"tags":["US","slang"],"topics":["government","politics"]}],"upstream_line":10450231,"upstream_offset":25263010613,"upstream_record_sha256":"d078f66af2b8bcf95fba9a7f2ea4a9075df33255c4d52510c7c524cb9bdabedd","word":"MAGA Mike"}

{"forms":[{"form":"dative cases","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Case used to express direction towards an indirect object, the recipient or beneficiary of an action, and is generally indicated in English by to (when a recipient) or for (when a beneficiary) with the objective case. The direct object may be either stated or unstated where the indirect object is the beneficiary of the verbal action, but is stated where the indirect object is a recipient. If there is emphasis on the indirect object, the indirect object usually precedes the direct object and is not usually indicated with to or for; said structure is also used when neither object is being emphasized."],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":16778,"upstream_offset":113218828,"upstream_record_sha256":"a84017ce20d29946f15a63940602295ebf36193756be520f7b9c950d1755514f","word":"dative case"}

{"forms":[{"form":"dovetails","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"dovetailing","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"dovetailed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"dovetailed","tags":["past"]},{"form":"dove-tail","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To unite (components) with a dovetail (noun sense 2.1) or similar joint."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["arts","business","carpentry","construction","crafts","hobbies","lifestyle","manufacturing","woodworking"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The executive board dovetailed its decision neatly with the prior projects the company had taken up.","type":"example"},{"text":"Through my new project, I dovetail my interests in botany and programming.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To combine or fit (things) together well."],"tags":["figuratively","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To interweave (a number of algorithms or subprograms) so that they can be run more or less simultaneously."],"tags":["figuratively","transitive"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"glosses":["To seamlessly move a melody from one instrument to another."],"tags":["figuratively","transitive"],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The parts of your essay should dovetail so that it is cohesive and coherent.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of several things: to combine or fit together well."],"tags":["figuratively","intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To adapt to something; to fit in."],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈdʌvteɪl/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈdʌvˌteɪl/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":528798,"upstream_offset":2430485010,"upstream_record_sha256":"0286414139e1c5edf8d008f61971b78aa421ada0cba9adf32333bb6eda72b1c7","word":"dovetail"}

{"forms":[{"form":"ultrafilters","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A device that performs ultrafiltration."]},{"examples":[{"text":"A proper filter of a set has these three properties: (1) it does not contain the empty set, (2) if it contains a subset then it contains all supersets of that subset, and (3) if it contains a pair of subsets then it also contains their intersection. To make it into an ultrafilter it must be made as large as possible without including the empty set. That can be prevented by not allowing any pair of disjoint sets to be both included. If, given a pair of complementary subsets, one of them is prevented from being included, then all subsets of it should be prevented from being included as well, by the second rule. That takes care of all subsets disjoint from the other complementary subset, which should then be included, in order to make the filter approach maximality, i.e., turn it into an ultrafilter.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A proper filter which has a law of dichotomy for complements."],"qualifier":"whose subsets are partially ordered by inclusion","raw_tags":["of a set"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"An ultrafilter is maximal in the sense that if any other element of the poset not already in it were added to it, one could deduce (from the laws which define the filter, and the given ordering relation, i.e., the structure of the poset) that the resulting filter must be improper; i.e., it must contain all the elements of the poset.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A filter (subset of a poset) that is maximal as a set with respect to the definition of proper filter."],"topics":["mathematics","order-theory","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":2161903,"upstream_offset":6829323015,"upstream_record_sha256":"cfc8dadd80f3d83e02a8daf4a9da7402bee002ff571269367a8aca30484806c5","word":"ultrafilter"}
{"forms":[{"form":"ultrafilters","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"ultrafiltering","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"ultrafiltered","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"ultrafiltered","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To filter by ultrafiltration."],"tags":["transitive"]}],"upstream_line":2161904,"upstream_offset":6829327115,"upstream_record_sha256":"4c6df3a08199af395f8de34873a542f35dd632234d76c2f2446f8ae4aa999da2","word":"ultrafilter"}

{"forms":[{"form":"updates","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I just made an update to my blog about my trip to Rome.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An additional piece of information, an addition to existing information."]},{"examples":[{"text":"He gave me an update on the situation in New York.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An additional piece of information, an addition to existing information.","An advisement providing more up-to-date information than currently known."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Our database receives an update every morning at 3 AM.","type":"example"},{"text":"I have a couple of updates to install on your laptop.","type":"example"},{"text":"I just made an update to the Wikipedia article on guerrillas.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A change in information, a modification of existing or known data.","A modification of something to a more recent, up-to-date version; (in software) a minor upgrade."]},{"examples":[{"text":"stochastic update","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A change in information, a modification of existing or known data.","An adjustment of parameters in a model, or the rule by which they are adjusted."],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences","statistics"]},{"examples":[{"text":"You should try the update: it rocks.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A version of something which is newer than other versions."],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences","software"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"ŭp'dāt"},{"enpr":"ŭp-dāt'"},{"ipa":"/ˈʌp.deɪt/"},{"ipa":"/ʌpˈdeɪt/"}],"upstream_line":19121,"upstream_offset":134691813,"upstream_record_sha256":"9a4ca1dbc1b51634a9ef21fbfbc7c6d2c458176165de021bb8c594009e081290","word":"update"}
